## ✅ **Playwright pre-existing failures: root-caused and fixed (2026-10-21)**

Not caused by the window-bridge work (same 3 failures at `2a02ebb`, before Phase 1).

- **Cause:** `isCommandAvailable` in `src/server/helpers/command-execution-helpers.js` (and `check-deps.js`) runs `spawnSync("bash", ["-lc", "command -v <cmd>"])`, a *login* shell per required command, on the request path. `getDependencyStatus()` is called synchronously by `POST /run-auto`. On a machine whose login shell is slow (here ~1.4s per spawn, measured: `bash -lc` 1.4s vs `bash -c` 8ms), the route blocks the whole server event loop for ~5.9s.
- **Trigger auto run test:** the route answers 202 after 5.9s; the test allows 5s for the button to settle. Fails deterministically on this machine, also with `gh` unauthenticated (ruled out `gh` network time).
- **Quick check over SSE:** failed while the server was blocked by the same login-shell spawns (and on a cold Vite cache); passes now.
- **PR JSON modal (separate real bug, found while chasing the above):** `PrJsonModal`'s first render after opening has `loading=false` and no `detailsPayload` yet (the open effect sets both), so it flashed empty JSON blocks for a frame. The test read the blocks in that window (`toBeVisible` plus `not.toHaveText("Loading...")` both pass on an empty block), hence `Unexpected end of JSON input`, only in the full run. Fixed in the component: no payload yet counts as loading.
- **Not caused by:** `gh` speed (0.4-1.1s), the dotfiles `PATH` change (sourcing `common.sh` is ~1.4s before and after), or any Phase 1/2 change.
- **Fix 1 (dependency check):** `isCommandAvailable` (`command-execution-helpers.js`, and the copy in `check-deps.js`) looks the command up on the process `PATH` in Node first (regular executable file, plain command names only), and only falls back to the `bash -lc` login-shell check when that misses, so a command that is only on the login shell's `PATH` still counts (the scripts themselves run under `bash -lc`). `check-deps.test.js` now sets an empty `PATH` so its mocked login-shell fallback decides, instead of depending on what the test machine has installed.
- **Fix 2 (`PrJsonModal`):** see above.
- **Tests:** +4 for the `PATH` lookup (found without a shell; fallback result; non-executable/directory ignored; path-separator names never match), +1 first-render "Loading..." regression test for `PrJsonModal` (fails without the fix). Jest 2493/2493 (was 2488), eslint clean, Playwright 55/55 on three consecutive runs (was 52/55; suite ~40s, was ~60s).

## ✅ **Memory leak in the filter-match cache (Chrome "Error code: 5" renderer crash) - present in the latest code, fixed (2026-10-21)**

Reported against an older version: the page periodically crashed with "This page is having a problem / Error code: 5" (renderer out of memory), nothing in the terminal.

- **Still present.** `applyRowUiFilters` (`pr-row-filtering.helpers.js`) cached each entry's filter-match result under `filterMatch:<JSON of all criteria>` via `createEntryDerivedCache().getOrCompute`. That cache's `WeakMap` only frees an entry's `Map` when the *entry object* dies, but entries keep the same reference across renders while their data is unchanged, so the per-entry `Map` was never pruned. In `useFilteredPrRows`, only `filterPrNumbers` is debounced; the 6 free-text fields (`filterCustomComments`, `filterOtherNotes`, `filterPrDifficulty`, `filterRallyStories`, `filterRallyLinks`, `filterAnalysisOfPr`) flow straight into the criteria, so every keystroke created a new fingerprint and one permanent cache key on every loaded PR. N keystrokes x M PRs entries, unbounded.
- **Fix.** `createEntryDerivedCache` gains `getOrComputeLatest(entry, key, variant, compute)`, which keeps only the latest variant per (entry, key) and overwrites it when the variant changes (cost: one recompute when switching back to an earlier criteria set, never growth), plus `getCachedKeyCount(entry)` for tests/diagnostics. `applyRowUiFilters` uses it (key `filterMatch`, variant = criteria fingerprint); `useFilteredPrRows` passes it in. The other `getOrCompute` callers use fixed keys (`fingerprint`, `labelNames`, ...) and don't grow. `getOrCompute` stays for them; a caller wiring only `getOrCompute` into the filtering helper still gets the old unbounded behavior, which the helper's comment now says.
- **Not changed:** the free-text fields are still undebounced (typing re-filters on every keystroke); that's a UX/perf choice separate from the leak, which is now bounded either way.
- **Regression tests (+18):** cache level (5: bounded at 1 key after 2000 variants x 50 entries; old key-per-variant approach grows, documenting why; unrelated keys untouched), filtering-helper level (7: typing into each of the 6 fields stays at 1 key per entry with correct results, plus cached-result reuse), and the real hook (`useVisiblePrNumbers.test.jsx`, 6: typing into each field through `useFilteredPrRows`, so it also proves the wiring). Verified they fail without the fix: reverting only the filtering helper fails 12, reverting only the hook wiring fails 6.
- **Follow-up (found reviewing this change): `PrJsonModal` stale reopen flash.** The earlier "no payload yet means loading" fix only covered the very first open. After a close, the component keeps its state, so reopening (for a different PR) rendered the *previous* PR's JSON and title for one frame. The open effect's cleanup now clears `detailsPayload`/`diffData`, so a reopen starts from "loading". +1 test (reopen as a different PR; fails without the cleanup).
- **Verification:** eslint clean; jest 2512/2512 (2511 after the cache fix, 2493 before it); Playwright 55/55.

## ✅ **Second "Error code: 5" leak: PrTableApp row handlers chained every past payload - fixed (2026-10-21)**

The crash came back after the filter-match fix above, so that leak was real but not the whole story.

- **Found by measurement, not reading.** A scratch Playwright/CDP harness (forced GC before each sample, 200 PRs, fast poll interval) ran three scenarios separately. Idle polling and typing into the 6 free-text filters stayed flat. Changing the server's data file between polls grew the heap linearly by about 0.64MB per data change (29MB to 67MB over 60 changes), with DOM nodes and listeners constant. A heap snapshot's retainer path for an old PR title went `<tr>` fiber, then `onCheckboxChange`, then alternating `handleCheckboxChange`/`handleAckAction`/`handleAckActionBusy` closure scopes, then each scope's `payload`.
- **Cause.** V8 gives all closures created in one render a single shared scope. `PrTableApp`'s row handlers were `useCallback`s with *different* deps: `handleCheckboxChange` changed on every payload (via `useRowCheckboxActions`, whose memo depends on `payload`), the others on busy state/`selectedRepo`. Each new handler's scope held the still-memoized sibling from an older render, whose scope held an older sibling, and so on, so every past `payload` stayed reachable from the memoized rows. In real use every auto-refresh and every other tab's `data-changed` event is a data change, and real payloads are far larger than the fixture, so a long-open tab eventually exhausted the renderer.
- **Fix.** New `state/useStableCallback.jsx` (the `useEvent` pattern: a stable function that calls the latest callback through a ref, updated in a layout effect). `PrTableApp`'s 7 row handlers (`handleCheckboxChange`, `handleAckAction`, `handleApplyLabel`, `withPrBusy`, and the 3 `*Busy` wrappers) use it instead of `useCallback`, so no older render scope is reachable from them. `PrRow` memoization is at least as effective as before (the identities never change now).
- **Result.** Same harness after the fix: 29MB to 31.5MB over 60 data changes, flat from step 10 on.
- **Regression tests (+4):** `useStableCallback.test.jsx` (3: stable identity, calls the latest callback, no-callback safe) and a `PrTableApp.test.jsx` test that the 4 row handler props keep their identity across 4 fresh payloads (fails without the fix).
- **Follow-up: `npm run stability:heap-check` now covers this.** It could not have caught the leak (it never changed the server's data), and on Windows it failed to start (spawned `npx` without a shell). It now runs the server and Vite on its own Node binary, shortens the page's data poll interval, and each cycle (`--data-change-every=N`, default 1, 0 disables) rewrites the data file with ~10% of PRs changed and waits for the page to fetch it, reporting any it never fetched. Default run (200 cycles, 40 PRs): 1.72x heap growth with the pre-fix `PrTableApp.jsx` (flagged), 1.11x with the fix (flat). It now exits 1 above `MAX_GROWTH_RATIO` (1.5x), or when any data change goes unfetched for 10s (a flat heap from a run that never refreshed the payload proves nothing), and runs on demand in CI from the Actions tab via `.github/workflows/heap-stability.yml` (inputs: cycles, dataset size, data-change interval); it is not part of the per-push Tests workflow.
- **Verification:** eslint clean; jest 2587 passed / 1 skipped; Playwright 59/59.

Plan: [WINDOW_BRIDGE_ELIMINATION_PLAN.md](WINDOW_BRIDGE_ELIMINATION_PLAN.md). Phase 3 is split into 3a (this), 3b (the 6 hooks' `beginRequestActivity`/`renderRequestActivity`), 3c (output text), 3d (backfill button state), because investigating it showed the plan's wording ("`AppRoot`'s locally-owned setters, thread as parameters") doesn't fit every caller.

Added `getHasDirtyPrSectionsFields`/`getPendingAutoRenderPayload`/`setPendingAutoRenderPayload` as stable `useCallback`s that read the Provider's ref (never render state), with inert defaults in the Context.
- `PrDataProvider` uses them (and lists them in `pollForDataChanges`' `useMemo` deps - stable, so no rebuild). Its 3 `window.*` reads are gone.

The real provider now sits in `FilterStateTestRootHarness` as the ancestor of the portaled table tree, mirroring `mountAppRoot()`; `installAutoRenderBlockingProviderBridge` was removed.

Plan: [WINDOW_BRIDGE_ELIMINATION_PLAN.md](WINDOW_BRIDGE_ELIMINATION_PLAN.md).

The integration harness needed no change: no test there asserts `#output`, and `index.page.js`'s writes still reach it through the harness's bridge.

## ✅ **Window-bridge elimination, Phase 3d: Backfill button state moves into `BackfillStatusProvider` (2026-10-21)**

Plan: [WINDOW_BRIDGE_ELIMINATION_PLAN.md](WINDOW_BRIDGE_ELIMINATION_PLAN.md). Completes Phase 3.

- `backfillButtonState` (the 4 button-disabled flags) was `AppRoot`'s local state, pushed in by `BackfillStatusProvider` (and `useBackfillActions`) through `window.updateReactBackfillButtonState` - an ancestor writing down into its own descendant through `window`. It now lives in `BackfillStatusProvider` and is exposed on `BackfillStatusContext` (`backfillButtonState`, a stable `setBackfillButtonState`; `DEFAULT_BACKFILL_BUTTON_STATE` exported). The Provider calls its own setter when a status render computes the view model; `useBackfillActions` takes the setter from Context; `AppRoot` reads `backfillButtonState` back for the buttons' props. Pulls forward the Phase 4 "extend `BackfillStatusProvider`'s Context" idea for this one value.
- **Bridge kept:** `window.updateReactBackfillButtonState` is still published, by the Provider now, for `index.page.js` (the one remaining caller; Phase 7).
- **Tests:** new `BackfillStatusProvider.test.jsx` (+6, the Provider had none: default state; a real `renderBackfillStatus` for a running backfill yields exactly the view model's button state, with a guard that it differs from the default; setter incl. falsy reset; vanilla bridge write; stable setter identity; bridge removed on unmount). `useBackfillActions.test.jsx` passes `setBackfillButtonState` through `BackfillStatusContext`. The integration harness's `BackfillControlsTestHarness` reads the Context instead of keeping local state fed by the bridge.
- Jest 2526/2526 (was 2520), eslint clean, Playwright 56/56.

### Phase 3 summary
Done: 3a (auto-render blocking via Context; 2 dead bridges deleted), 3b (6 hooks use `usePrData()` for request activity), 3c (output text -> `OutputTextProvider`), 3d (backfill button state -> `BackfillStatusProvider`). **Deliberately left (a real caller remains, none of them fixable from React):** `window.beginRequestActivity`/`renderRequestActivity` (callers: `index.page.js`; the latter also `PrDataProvider`'s own `pollForDataChanges` closure and the two providers that sit outside it), `window.updateReactOutputText` and `updateReactBackfillButtonState` (`index.page.js`), `window.updateReactAutoRenderBlockedLinks` (called only from vanilla code via `pr-auto-render-indicator-links.helpers.js`), and everything `index.page.js` owns (`applyFreshPrPayload`, `loadStoredData`, `getLatestStoredPayload`, `applyLatestPrData`, `summarizeAckRefreshWarnings`) - all Phase 7.

## ✅ **Phase 3 review: three render-cascade regressions I introduced, measured and fixed (2026-10-21)**

Found re-reading Phase 3 for what each new Context read subscribes a component to. Each was measured (a `React.memo` consumer's render count) before and after, and each fix has a regression test that fails when the fix is undone.

1. **`PrDataProvider` subscribed to `AutoRenderBlockingContext` (3a).** That Provider re-renders on every `pushAutoRenderBlockingState`, which vanilla's `recomputeDirtyOnEvent` fires on every input/change/click in the PR table, and its `value` wasn't memoized. `PrDataProvider`'s own value isn't either, so each push re-rendered every `usePrData()` consumer: **20 pushes -> 20 extra renders** (0 at `dcb8db8`, before Phase 3a). Fix: the three stable functions moved to their own `AutoRenderBlockingActionsContext` (`useAutoRenderBlockingActions()`), which `PrDataProvider` reads; the state value is `useMemo`'d, so portal consumers only re-render on real changes.
2. **The output-text writers subscribed to the text (3c).** `useSinglePrUpdate` runs inside `PrTableApp`, so each output write (every Run/Ack/Backfill action) re-rendered the table (**5 writes -> 5 extra renders**). Fix: the stable `setOutputText` has its own `OutputTextSetterContext` (`useSetOutputText()`); only `AppRoot` reads the text.
3. **`AppRoot`-level hooks subscribed to all of `PrDataProvider` (3b).** `useRunScript`/`useAckLabelActions`/`useBackfillActions`/`useRequestMoreMerged` (called from `AppRoot`'s body, which never subscribed to PR data before) and `NotesSection` read two never-changing functions through `usePrData()`, so `AppRoot` and every portal it builds re-rendered on each payload/repo/stats update (**5 updates -> 5 extra renders**). Fix: new `RequestActivityContext` (`useRequestActivity()`), published by `PrDataProvider` from its `activityHelpers` memo; the six consumers use it. `useRowCheckboxActions` keeps `usePrData()` (it needs `payload`/`selectedRepo` anyway).

The rule this keeps: a component that only needs stable functions from a changing Provider reads them from a stable, separate Context. `PrDataProvider`'s un-memoized `value` (the amplifier in 1 and 3) is pre-existing and untouched.

- Tests: +8 (ARB: actions-Context test replaced, +2 cascade tests under a real `PrDataProvider`; `OutputTextProvider` +3: setter-only consumer never re-renders, setter identity, inert default; `useRequestMoreMerged` +3: no re-render on PR data updates, stable instances, inert default). Existing tests that supplied a `PrDataContext` just for the two functions now supply `RequestActivityContext`. Verified each regression test fails against the broken version.
- Jest 2534/2534, eslint clean, Playwright 56/56.

## ✅ **Window-bridge elimination, Phase 4: `BackfillStatusProvider` owns all backfill status state and exposes its functions on a stable Context (2026-10-21)**

Plan: [WINDOW_BRIDGE_ELIMINATION_PLAN.md](WINDOW_BRIDGE_ELIMINATION_PLAN.md).

- **Functions:** `getBackfillTrackingState` (now also returns `isBackfillActionPending`), `updateBackfillStatusFromPayload`, `renderBackfillStatus`, `setBackfillLogMessage`, plus `setBackfillButtonState`/`setIsBackfillActionPending`/`setSupportsBackfillLogPolling` (moved over from Phase 1/3d), are on a new **`BackfillStatusActionsContext`** (`useBackfillStatusActions()`): every one a `useCallback` over refs/`useState` setters, so the object and each function keep one identity for the Provider's lifetime. `BackfillStatusContext` (`useBackfillStatus()`) is now state only. Split because of the Phase 3 review's lesson: `PrDataProvider` needs `getBackfillTrackingState` for its backfill poll and wraps the whole table tree, so it must not subscribe to state that changes on every status/log update (verified: the regression test fails if it does).
- **State moved in:** the 3 values the Provider's status render used to push into `AppRoot` through `window.updateReactBackfillBadges`/`DetailsText`/`LogText` (an ancestor writing into its own descendant) are Provider state now, read back by `AppRoot` for its portals. `backfillLogUpdateTick` bumps on every log write even for identical text (BackfillLogControls' scroll-sync effect keys on it).
- **Callers switched:** `useBackfillActions` (all remaining `window.updateBackfillStatusFromPayload`/`renderBackfillStatus`/`setBackfillLogMessage` calls) and `PrDataProvider.pollBackfillStatus` (`window.getBackfillTrackingState`) use the actions Context. `BackfillLogControls` confirmed consistent: it reads `isBackfillRunning` from the (unchanged) state Context.
- **Bridges deleted:** `window.updateReactBackfillBadges`, `window.updateReactBackfillDetailsText` (no caller left but the Provider itself).
- **Bridges kept, for `index.page.js` only (Phase 7):** `getBackfillTrackingState`, `setSupportsBackfillLogPolling`, `setIsBackfillActionPending`, `updateBackfillStatusFromPayload`, `setBackfillLogMessage`, `renderBackfillStatus`, `updateReactBackfillLogText` (its react-ready replay), `updateReactBackfillButtonState`. Their install effect is now one `useEffect` over the stable functions instead of per-render closures.
- **Tests:** `BackfillStatusProvider.test.jsx` rewritten for the split (+6, 12 total: owned state, status render == view-model output with vacuous-test guards, log tick on identical text, announce-once, stale-reference tracking reads, stable identities, inert defaults, cascade regression under a real `PrDataProvider`, bridges present/absent/removed); `PrDataProvider.test.jsx` +2 (pending action, no provider); `useBackfillActions.test.jsx` supplies the actions Context; integration harness reads log/details text from the real Provider instead of local state fed by bridges (and portals the details text like `AppRoot`); +1 Playwright test (details and log text replace their placeholders through the real Provider; fails when the details write is broken).
- Jest 2542/2542 (was 2534), eslint clean, Playwright 57/57 (was 56).

## ✅ **Phase 4 review: a latent refetch-loop hazard guarded, stale comments fixed (2026-10-21)**

Re-reading Phase 4 (and Phases 1-3 with it) for what could go wrong that no existing test would catch.

- **Latent hazard, not currently triggered: an unstable dependency would turn the Backfill tab into a request loop.** `useBackfillTabAutoRefresh` lists `useBackfillActions()`'s `triggerBackfillTabLoad` as an effect dependency and re-fetches status+log whenever it changes while the tab is visible; each fetch updates state that re-renders the caller. `useBackfillActions`' `useMemo` now depends on ~15 functions from 6 providers, so one function losing its `useCallback` anywhere in that chain gives the hook a new identity every render. Verified: with a single dependency made unstable, the real browser sent **498 status/log requests in 3 seconds** (1-5 normally). It is stable today (measured across status, output, backfill, snackbar and PR-data updates under the real providers), but nothing guarded it. Added: a jest test pinning the hook's result identity under the real providers and every kind of update that re-renders its caller (`useBackfillActions.test.jsx`, fails when a dependency loses its `useCallback`), and a Playwright test counting real status/log requests after opening the Backfill tab and idling 3 s (fails with `Received: 498`).
- **Coverage gap closed:** no test exercised the real chain `BackfillStatusProvider` -> `PrDataProvider.pollBackfillStatus`; the integration harness mounts them as separate roots, and the unit tests mock the tracking value. Added a test wiring the two real Providers in `mountAppRoot()`'s order: a running status render makes the poll reload status, a pending action or a stopped backfill suppresses it.
- **Stale comments fixed** (they described deleted bridges or contradicted the code): `BackfillStatusProvider`'s header paragraph said `updateReactBackfillBadges`/`DetailsText` "stay exactly as they are", `BackfillStatusContext`'s header, `BackfillControls`' header, and three comments in `index.page.js`. No behavior change.
- **Unrelated flake seen once, not fixed:** `src/server/integration-tests/app.events-routes.test.js` failed once in a full parallel run and then passed 4/4 alone and 3/3 more full runs. Its heartbeat test uses a 50 ms interval and a fixed 100 ms wait, so a loaded machine can miss the window. Pre-existing; left alone.
- Tests: +2 jest (identity guard, real-wiring), +1 Playwright. Jest 2544/2544, eslint clean, Playwright 58/58.

## ✅ **CI failure on Windows: the `PATH`-first dependency check is unsafe there (2026-10-21)**

Run `39663610747` (push of `dcb8db8`): lint, ubuntu `test` and `e2e` passed; **`test (windows-latest)`** failed with 2 tests. Not a flake: both trace to the `isCommandAvailable` `PATH`-lookup I added in `03dadc1` (see "Playwright pre-existing failures").

1. **`run-bash-script.test.js` "given bash is unavailable..." (a real bug, not just a test problem).** The test strips every `bin` directory from `PATH` to simulate "no bash". On the runner `C:\Windows\System32\bash.exe` is the **WSL launcher stub** (present with no distro installed, can't run a script) and isn't stripped. Before my change the check ran `bash -lc "command -v bash"`, the stub failed, and bash was correctly reported unavailable. With a `PATH` shortcut, merely *finding* the stub made it report "available", so `run-bash-script.js` skipped its actionable "requires bash ... Git Bash" message and the user got an empty failure. This is exactly the trade-off flagged earlier ("a command found on `PATH` counts without checking the login shell"); on Windows a file on `PATH` can be a non-functional stub.
2. **`command-execution-helpers.test.js` "file is not executable".** My test was POSIX-only: Windows has no executable bit, so `accessSync(X_OK)` succeeds for any readable file.

**Fix:** the `PATH` shortcut never runs on Windows (`process.platform === "win32"` -> always the real `bash -lc` check, i.e. exactly the previous behavior there); the not-executable assertion is skipped on a real Windows filesystem. Both `command-execution-helpers.js` and `check-deps.js`.

**A second regression I introduced while fixing it, caught by running the exact CI command, not bare `jest`:** simplifying the lookup (dropping the Windows extension loop) replaced `path.join(dir, \`${cmd}${ext}\`)`, which coerced `null` to `"null"`, with `path.join(dir, cmd)`, which throws on a non-string; `app.scheduler-timing.test.js` "isCommandAvailable handles null input" failed (deterministically, 3/3). The name is now `typeof`-checked before the regex (a bare regex `.test` coerces `null`/`undefined`/numbers to strings and passes). My earlier full `jest` run predated that edit, which is why `npm run lint:check`, `npm run test:ci` and `npm run test:e2e` were run as CI does.

- Tests: +3 `command-execution-helpers` (Windows with a file of that name on `PATH` still runs the real check; non-string names x3 via `test.each`; the not-executable and directory cases split), +2 `check-deps` (POSIX shortcut vs Windows, non-string names). Each new test verified to fail against the pre-fix source; the `check-deps` non-string test was first vacuous (that file's empty `PATH` meant nothing was ever joined) and was fixed to use a non-empty `PATH`.
- Verified locally (macOS): `lint:check` clean; `test:ci` 2552/2552 with the state guards; `test:e2e` 58/58. **Not verified on Windows itself** - no Windows runner here; the Windows behavior is pinned by injecting `platform: "win32"` and by the unchanged pre-existing code path, and needs the next CI run to confirm.

## ✅ **Window-bridge elimination, Phase 5a: `PrSelectionCell` reads `prNumbersInput` through a per-key `useSyncExternalStore` store (2026-10-21)**

Plan: [WINDOW_BRIDGE_ELIMINATION_PLAN.md](WINDOW_BRIDGE_ELIMINATION_PLAN.md).

- **The problem the window bridge was dodging:** `FilterStateContext`'s `value` is one object over 25+ independent fields whose `values` gets a new reference on every single-field change, and `useFilterState()` has no selector. `PrSelectionCell` mounts once per visible table row, so a real subscription would re-render every row on every keystroke in any filter field. It read `prNumbersInput` non-reactively through `window.getFilterStateValues()`/`setFilterStateValue()` instead.
- **New:** `state/filterValuesStore.jsx` - a plain, per-`FilterStateProvider`-instance external store (`getAll`/`getSnapshot(key)`/`setValue`/`setMany`/`subscribe(key, fn)`; immutable updates; nothing module-global). `FilterStateProvider` keeps it mirroring `values`: `setValue` and the restore-on-mount effect update both in the same call, so they cannot diverge (the whole-object `values` Context every other consumer uses is untouched). It publishes `FilterFieldStoreContext` (`{ store, actions }`, one identity for the Provider's lifetime). `useFilterFieldValue(key, select)` (a `useSyncExternalStore` with an optional selector) and `useFilterFieldActions()` (stable `getFieldValue`/`setFieldValue`) are in `FilterStateContext.jsx`; both are inert with no Provider.
- **`PrSelectionCell`** subscribes with a selector returning just *its own* boolean (`parse(input).includes(prNumber)`), so a row re-renders only when its own membership flips - measured in tests: typing into 20 other fields -> 0 row re-renders; selecting one more PR of 25 -> exactly 1; editing the field without changing anyone's membership -> 0 (the naive `useFilterState()` version fails all three). The checkbox is now genuinely controlled (`checked`, not `defaultChecked`).
- **A latent bug this fixes:** typing PR numbers into the Run tab's "PR number(s)" field never updated the row checkboxes (the one-way `defaultChecked` read happened once at mount). New e2e test; fails against the old cell with `Expected: checked, Received: unchecked`.
- **Deliberately not done here:** `window.getFilterStateValues`/`setFilterStateValue` are NOT deleted. `PrSelectionCell` was their only *production* caller (`index.page.js` has none, despite the cell's old comment), but ~15 test files still use them for assertions/setup; deleting them is a mechanical test rewrite, tracked as 5b.
- **Tests (+23 jest, +1 e2e):** `filterValuesStore.test.jsx` (12: store semantics + the hooks incl. selector bail-out, resubscribe on key change, inert defaults), `PrSelectionCell.test.jsx` rewritten against the real Provider (10 incl. the 3 render-cost tests), `FilterStateProvider.test.jsx` +6 (store and values in sync via every write path, restore-on-mount included, stable context identity). Each sync path verified by breaking it. `lint:check`, `test:ci` (2575/2575 + state guards) and `test:e2e` (59/59) all pass.

## ✅ **Window-bridge elimination, Phase 5b: `window.getFilterStateValues`/`setFilterStateValue` deleted (2026-10-21)**

Plan: [WINDOW_BRIDGE_ELIMINATION_PLAN.md](WINDOW_BRIDGE_ELIMINATION_PLAN.md). Follows 5a, which moved their only production caller (`PrSelectionCell`) to the per-key store.

- **Confirmed by grep before deleting:** `index.page.js` never called either bridge (the old `PrSelectionCell` comment claiming it did was wrong); the only remaining users were ~15 test files. Per the standing policy for test-only bridges, the tests were fixed instead of keeping the bridge.
- **Deleted** from `FilterStateProvider` (with its `flushSync` import). `window.persistViewFilterOptionOverrides` is untouched (Phase 6).
- **Component tests (11 files)** read and write the state through a new shared helper, `test-fixtures/filter-state-probe.jsx` (`createFilterStateProbe()`: a `<FilterStateProbe />` mounted inside the real `FilterStateProvider`, with `readValues()`/`setValue()`), so they exercise the same Context the components read. `useAppliedFilterSummary.test.jsx` seeds through it too.
- **`FilterStateProvider.test.jsx`:** the bridge-specific tests (write through the bridge, synchronous-read-after-write, bridges removed on unmount) are gone since there is no bridge to test; the value-change test asserts through a Context reader; +1 test that neither bridge is published.
- **Integration harness (`index.html.test.js`):** `syncFilterStateFromElement` (68 call sites) and 3 assertions go through `filterStateTestHandle`, the real Provider's Context value captured by a `FilterStateTestHandle` component in the harness root, with `flushSync` to keep the synchronous visibility the bridge gave.

## ✅ **Phase 5 review: `useFilterFieldValue` crashed on a selector returning a new object; fixed (2026-10-21)**

Re-reading Phase 5 for what the new API allows that its only caller doesn't exercise.

- **Defect:** `useSyncExternalStore` needs `getSnapshot` to return the same value while the store is unchanged, and my hook ran the caller's selector on every snapshot read. A selector that builds a fresh array/object (`raw.split(',')`) therefore crashed with **`Maximum update depth exceeded` after 109 selector calls**. Not triggered today (the only caller, `PrSelectionCell`, returns a boolean), but it is an exported general hook.
- **Fix:** the selected value is cached per (raw value, selector) in a ref, so repeated reads within a render return the identical result and the selector runs once per render or store change instead of once per snapshot read (the same idea as React's `use-sync-external-store/with-selector`, without taking a transitive dependency). Documented on the hook: prefer primitives or a `useCallback`'d selector, since a fresh object still re-renders on each raw change.
- **Checked and fine:** subscriptions are balanced across a key change and unmount (2 subscribes, 2 unsubscribes); a primitive selector runs once per store change; the store/`values` mirror is only written in the two places `setValues` is called (covered by tests that break each path); the field-store Context keeps one identity.
- **Behavior note, an improvement:** a PR that appears in several sections (e.g. "open" and "in-review") has several rows with the same checkbox. They now all follow the field; with the old one-way `defaultChecked`, only the clicked one changed.
- Tests: +3 (`filterValuesStore.test.jsx`: new-array selector doesn't loop and re-renders once per change; bounded selector calls; subscription balance). The first two fail against the previous hook. `lint:check`, `test:ci` (2575/2575 + state guards) and `test:e2e` (59/59) pass locally; not yet pushed, so the Windows CI job has not run on Phase 5.

## ✅ **Window-bridge elimination, Phase 6: `window.persistViewFilterOptionOverrides` deleted; tests wait for the real 150 ms debounce (2026-10-21)**

Plan: [WINDOW_BRIDGE_ELIMINATION_PLAN.md](WINDOW_BRIDGE_ELIMINATION_PLAN.md). The bridge had no production callers; tests used it to force a save instead of waiting for `FilterStateProvider`'s debounced effect.

- **Deleted** the installing `useEffect` and its comments from `FilterStateProvider.jsx`; the header comment now says every save comes from the debounced effect.
- **Deviation: real timers, not fake ones, in `index.html.test.js` (11 call sites).** The plan said to scope `jest.useFakeTimers()` to each test and advance 150 ms. That doesn't fit this file. Its tests run `userEvent.setup()` on real timers. And switching to fake timers mid-test leaves the earlier debounce timers real: the fake `clearTimeout` that a later change's effect cleanup calls can't cancel them, so stale PUTs would race the asserted one. Instead, a new helper, `waitForPersistedViewFilterOverrides(assertBody)`, waits for the *latest* `/view-prs/user-defaults` PUT and runs the body assertions inside `waitFor`. That tolerates an intermediate save if a test's steps straddle 150 ms, because the debounce always ends on the final state. `fetchMock.mockClear()` moved to *before* each stage's changes (it used to run after them, which would now discard the save). The two reset-stage checks gained one assertion each, so an intermediate save can't satisfy them early.
- **The "no change filter selections" test changed no field**, so with the bridge gone nothing would save, and nothing does in production either. It now checks and then unchecks a reviewer, so it still ends with every list empty (`changeFilters` omitted).
- **Stale test comments removed:** they described an `index.page.js` delegated "change" persist listener that was already deleted in H.26. Six test titles still said "when Apply filters clicked" (that button is long gone) and were renamed.
- **`FilterStateProvider.test.jsx`** (already on fake timers file-wide) follows the plan as written: it asserts no PUT yet, runs `advanceTimersByTimeAsync(150)`, then checks the PUT body.
- **Not in the plan: e2e.** `e2e/smoke.spec.js`'s `waitForFilterPersist` (38 call sites) also called the bridge. It now waits for the real save. First, a 200 ms timer *inside the page* (page timers fire in due order, so the debounce has fired by then). Then a Node-side tracker (`page.on('request'|'requestfinished'|'requestfailed')`, installed in the suite's `beforeEach`) waits until no user-defaults request is in flight and none has started or finished for 100 ms. That covers the GET→PUT gap inside one save. No app hook, and the call sites are unchanged.
- **Proved:** with the debounced `persistViewFilterOptions()` call commented out, 13 jest tests fail (the 11 rewritten integration tests, the unit test, and one existing persistence test); restored, all pass.
- `lint:check` clean; `test:ci` 2574 passed + 1 skipped (same tests as before, renamed only); `test:e2e` 59/59. Windows local run.

## ✅ **Window-bridge elimination, Phase 7.1: the data pipeline moves from `index.page.js` into `PrDataProvider`; 15 bridges deleted (2026-10-21)**

Plan: [WINDOW_BRIDGE_ELIMINATION_PLAN.md](WINDOW_BRIDGE_ELIMINATION_PLAN.md), Phase 7's first responsibility ("data-poll pipeline").

- **Moved into `state/PrDataProvider.jsx`:** `latestStoredPayload`/`latestSelectedRepo` (now refs: a deferred poll result can be ahead of the displayed payload, so they're not `state`), `applyLatestPrData`, `renderPrData`, `applyFreshPrPayload`, `loadStoredData` (same factory, same DI), and the fingerprint/manifest polling helpers (with their own derived-value cache). `pollForDataChanges` now uses these directly instead of `window.*`. `renderPrData` reads the Run & Filter `repo` field from `FilterStateProvider`'s per-key store (`useFilterFieldActions().getFieldValue`), not the DOM `<input>`, so the Provider doesn't subscribe to filter changes.
- **New `PrDataActionsContext`** (`loadStoredData`/`applyFreshPrPayload`/`applyLatestPrData`/`getLatestStoredPayload`), a separate stable Context per lesson 1. Its six consumers (`useAckLabelActions`, `useQuickCheckActions`, `useRequestMoreMerged`, `useRowCheckboxActions`, `useRunScript`, `useSinglePrUpdate`) read it instead of `window.*`. `summarizeAckRefreshWarnings` moved to `helpers/pr-ack-refresh-warnings.helpers.js` as a plain import.
- **`AutoRenderBlockingProvider` is `PrDataProvider`'s ancestor**, so it can't read that Context. `PrDataProvider` registers its `applyFreshPrPayload` through a new `setFreshPayloadApplier` on `AutoRenderBlockingActionsContext`, and the apply/discard paths call that instead of `window.applyFreshPrPayload`.
- **The initial page-load fetch** runs in `PrDataProvider`'s own mount effect. It's opt-in (`loadOnMount`, set by `mountAppRoot()`): dozens of component tests mount this Provider with their own `initialPayload`, which an unconditional fetch would overwrite with their fetch mock's response. Its failure path (status text, output, error snackbar) is the one `initPage` had.
- **Deviation: the table is gated, not "always rendered".** `AppRoot`'s new `PrTablePortal` mounts `PrTableApp` once `hasRenderedPrData` (new Provider state) is true, the same moment `renderPrData` used to call `window.mountReactPrTable`. Rendering it unconditionally would flash an empty table before the first fetch. `PrTablePortal` is its own component so only it, not `AppRoot`, subscribes to `PrDataContext`. One deliberate difference: an `applyFreshPrPayload` before the first load now shows the table, where `updateReactTable` used to drop it.
- **Deleted bridges (15):** `window.renderPrData`, `getLatestStoredPayload`, `applyLatestPrData`, `loadStoredData`, `applyFreshPrPayload`, `summarizeAckRefreshWarnings`, `computePrDataManifest`, `getManifestDelta`, `mergeDataDeltaPayload`, `computePrDataFingerprint`, `computePrDataMetaFingerprint`, `getDataPollRenderAction`, `pushPrDataPollingTrackingState`, `pollForDataChanges` (its callers already used Context), `mountReactPrTable`. With them went `index.page.js`'s `mountReactTable`/`updateReactTable`/`renderPrTableMountError` and its `viewprs:react-ready` re-render listener. `index.page.js` is 2100 → 1603 lines.
- **`index.page.js`'s actor-login aliases** (feeding `normalizeActorLogin`'s defaults in its auto-render-blocking helpers) used to be committed by `renderPrData` on every render. They're now derived on demand from `window.getReactPrTablePayload()` (the displayed payload), cached per payload. The cache is claimed before deriving, so the nested `normalizeActorLogin` call sees the previous aliases instead of recursing.
- **Integration harness (`index.html.test.js`):** its real `PrDataProvider` is hoisted to the root of `FilterStateTestRootHarness` with `loadOnMount`, nested like `mountAppRoot()`. The plan had scheduled this for "when `index.page.js` goes"; it's needed now because the Provider does the fetch. So: `WindowBackedPrActivityProvider` and the `mountReactPrTable` slot/stub are gone. `BackfillStatusProvider` and `BackfillControlsTestHarness` merged into the one root (a separate root can't receive the per-payload backfill status through Context). `installReactTableMountBridge` became `installReactTestGlobals`. The "React mount failure" test is deleted along with the vanilla mount-error branch it covered.
- **Tests:** +7 in `PrDataProvider.test.jsx`: load on mount (fetch, render, resolved repo, backfill push), no load without `loadOnMount`, the failure path, `applyFreshPrPayload`'s effects, `applyLatestPrData` not touching the display, applier register/unregister, and actions identity stable across payload updates with a `memo` consumer not re-rendering. Each failed against a deliberately broken Provider (payload-dependent actions memo, `loadOnMount` ignored, applier never registered, pending payload not cleared). With the applier unregistered, the integration suite's 2 auto-render-blocking poll tests fail too. `react-app.test.jsx` was rewritten for the new contract (no table until the first fetch resolves, then `PrTableApp` in `#pr-sections` with that payload; `updateReactPrTable`'s merge; the `viewprs:react-ready` ordering guard now checks `updateReactAutoRenderBlockedLinks`). Six hook tests and `AutoRenderBlockingProvider.test.jsx` provide the new Contexts instead of `window.*` stubs; `useRequestMoreMerged.test.jsx` now asserts its reload goes through Context.
- `lint:check` clean; `test:ci` 2580 passed + 1 skipped (2575 before, −1 deleted, +7 new); `test:e2e` 59/59. Windows local run.
- **Left for later Phase 7 steps:** `window.updateReactPrTable`/`getReactPrTablePayload`/`getReactPrTableSelectedRepo` (many tests use the first as a "push a payload" utility; `index.page.js`'s auto-render-blocking code reads the second), and `window.setStatusMessage`.

## ✅ **Window-bridge elimination, Phase 7.2: backfill status bootstrap leaves `index.page.js`; 13 bridges deleted (2026-10-21)**

Plan: [WINDOW_BRIDGE_ELIMINATION_PLAN.md](WINDOW_BRIDGE_ELIMINATION_PLAN.md), Phase 7's second responsibility ("backfill status bootstrap ... delete race-shim forwarders"). Also folds in the stale-comment fixes from the review of Phases 6-7.1.

- **The initial load was a duplicate, so it's deleted rather than moved.** `initPage`'s `loadBackfillStatus({ includeLog: true })` and its catch block were identical to `useBackfillActions().triggerBackfillTabLoad`, which `AppRoot`'s `useBackfillTabAutoRefresh` already fires on mount. So every page load fetched the backfill status and log twice. With it go the two `viewprs:react-ready` replays (`latestBackfillStatus`/`latestBackfillLogMessage`), which existed only because the vanilla load could finish before `BackfillStatusProvider` mounted. New integration test: a fresh page load fetches each exactly once (it fails with 2 against the old `index.page.js`).
- **`BackfillStatusProvider` builds its own `loadBackfillStatus`** (the same factory, load functions only) and exposes it on `BackfillStatusActionsContext`. `PrDataProvider`'s backfill poll reads it there instead of `window.loadBackfillStatus`. `BACKFILL_LOG_TAIL_LINES` moved to `BackfillStatusContext.jsx`, shared with `useBackfillActions` (it was duplicated there).
- **Deleted from `index.page.js`:** the `loadBackfillStatus` instance, the `setBackfillLogMessage`/`renderBackfillStatus`/`updateBackfillStatusFromPayload` forwarders and their caches, the backfill-log auto-scroll (`BackfillLogControls` already scrolls itself), the replays, and the helpers that only they used: `setStatusMessage`, `setStatusTextOnly`, `setOutputMessage`, the `beginRequestActivity` proxy, `showErrorNotification`/`notifyFailureSnackbar`, `postJson`, `formatCommandOutput`, `stripAnsi`, plus their imports. 1603 → 1326 lines.
- **Deleted bridges (13):** `window.getBackfillTrackingState`, `setSupportsBackfillLogPolling`, `setIsBackfillActionPending`, `updateBackfillStatusFromPayload`, `setBackfillLogMessage`, `updateReactBackfillLogText`, `renderBackfillStatus`, `updateReactBackfillButtonState`, `loadBackfillStatus`, `setStatusMessage` (no reader at all), `updateReactStatusText` (last caller was `setStatusTextOnly`), `beginRequestActivity` (last reader was the `index.page.js` proxy), `getLatestSchedulerState` (last reader was the `prDataTabOrchestrator` deleted in H.23). The integration harness's `installReactStatusTextMountBridges` stubs went too.
- **Now test-only (deferred to a dedicated step, like `setPendingAutoRenderPayload`):** `window.showErrorNotification`/`showWarningNotification`/`hideErrorNotification` (e2e Snackbar test) and `window.updateReactOutputText` (e2e output-panel test and its unit test). Their Providers' comments say so.
- **Ordering change found by the integration suite:** `AppRoot`'s backfill load now starts before `PrDataProvider`'s data load (child effects run first), so the data load's `updateBackfillStatusFromPayload(payload)` can be the last backfill render. In production that's fine: `/view-prs/data` embeds the same live backfill state (`buildDataPayload({ data, backfill })` in `view-prs-data-routes.js`). The test fetch mock didn't, so `payload.backfill || payload` rendered the PR payload as a blank status. The mock now embeds the same backfill state as `/view-prs/backfill`, like the server.
- **e2e: two fixes to the Phase 6 save-wait, found by a flaky run.** (1) Its in-flight counter could stick above zero forever: a user-defaults request cancelled by `page.reload()` reports neither `requestfinished` nor `requestfailed`. It now tracks a `Set` of requests, cleared on main-frame navigation. (2) Two restore tests ("Run Script options", "exclude-label/author/assigned/approver") cleaned up with a read-modify-write PUT that could race the restore's own debounced save, which writes the restored values back. One run leaked `pr-numbers: "912,921"` into the bulk-select test. Both now `waitForFilterPersist` before cleaning up, the same order the thread-resolution test already used. 3 consecutive full runs green afterwards, then 2 more on the final code.
- **Comment fixes** (from the review): `PrDataProvider`'s header (the Phase F/F.2 paragraphs claiming `window.pollForDataChanges`/`beginRequestActivity` were live), `PrDataPolling.jsx`, `pr-data-polling-orchestration.helpers.js`, `PrTableApp.jsx`, plus `StatusTextProvider`/`OutputTextProvider`/`SnackbarProvider`/`BackfillStatusContext` for this step's deletions.
- **Tests:** `BackfillStatusProvider.test.jsx`: the 3 bridge tests are replaced by 1 "publishes none of them" test; +4 `loadBackfillStatus` tests (status+log render, no log fetch without `includeLog`, a 404 log turning log polling off, a failed status rejecting with `error.result`); the Provider-wiring test now runs end to end on a fetch mock instead of a `window.loadBackfillStatus` stub. With the loader removed from the actions memo, 5 of them fail. `PrDataProvider.test.jsx`'s poll tests take the loader from Context; its request-activity tests read `useRequestActivity()` and assert the "Auto run" badge instead of `window.getLatestSchedulerState`.
- `lint:check` clean; `test:ci` 2583 passed + 1 skipped (2581 before: +2 Provider, +1 integration); `test:e2e` 59/59 (×5 after the fixes). Windows local run.

## ✅ **Window-bridge elimination, Phase 7.3: request activity and scheduler state move into a new `RequestActivityProvider`; 7 bridges deleted (2026-10-21)**

Plan: [WINDOW_BRIDGE_ELIMINATION_PLAN.md](WINDOW_BRIDGE_ELIMINATION_PLAN.md), Phase 7's third responsibility ("scheduler/request-activity CustomEvent dispatch -> PrDataProvider effect").

- **Deviation: a new top-level Provider, not `PrDataProvider`.** The request-activity code already lived in `PrDataProvider` (Phase F.2), and that was the problem: two of its callers, `BackfillStatusProvider` and `AutoRenderBlockingProvider`, are `PrDataProvider`'s *ancestors*, so they could only reach `renderRequestActivity` through `window`. The new `components/RequestActivityProvider.jsx` needs no other Context, so `mountAppRoot()` mounts it above all three (just inside `OutputTextProvider`), and every caller reads `useRequestActivity()`. The integration harness nests it the same way.
- **Moved in:** from `PrDataProvider`, `beginRequestActivity`/`renderRequestActivity` and their ref-held counters/history. From `index.page.js`, `renderSchedulerStatus` (scheduler state for the "Auto run" badge, plus the `pr-active-progress-update` event `PrTableApp` listens for) and the startup `loadSchedulerStatus()` (opt-in `loadOnMount`, as with `PrDataProvider`). `index.page.js` also set each row's `.pr-progress-indicator` `hidden` attribute directly; `PrNumberCell` already renders that from `PrTableApp`'s state, so it's dropped, not moved.
- **The badges and recent-activity list are this Provider's state now**, on a separate `RequestActivityDisplayContext` (lesson 1: it changes on every activity tick; the functions' Context never changes). `AppRoot` used to hold them as its own state, pushed in by `PrDataProvider` through `window.updateReactRequestActivityBadges`/`updateReactRecentRequestActivity`. The first state is the idle badge set, so `index.page.js`'s startup `renderRequestActivity()` and its `viewprs:react-ready` re-push go too.
- **`usePrData()` no longer carries `beginRequestActivity`/`renderRequestActivity`.** Its three readers (`AuthorInsightsDraftsProvider`, `useRowCheckboxActions`, `PrDataPolling`) use `useRequestActivity()`. `useQuickCheckActions` and `JobEventsProvider` read `renderSchedulerStatus` there too. `PrDataPolling` owns its `AUTO_DATA_POLL_MS`/`AUTO_BACKFILL_POLL_MS` as exported constants (it was their only reader).
- **Deleted bridges (7):** `window.renderRequestActivity`, `setLatestSchedulerStateAndRender`, `renderSchedulerStatus`, `updateReactRequestActivityBadges`, `updateReactRecentRequestActivity`, `AUTO_DATA_POLL_MS`, `AUTO_BACKFILL_POLL_MS`. `index.page.js` 1326 → 1191 lines; its only `viewprs:react-ready` listener left is the credential-hint one.
- **Tests:** new `RequestActivityProvider.test.jsx` (+6: idle badges on mount; begin/finish badges and a single recent entry, with the two tests moved from `PrDataProvider.test.jsx`; `renderSchedulerStatus` showing "Auto run" and dispatching the active PRs; `loadOnMount` loading once; no load without it, and a failed load ignored; stable actions with a `memo` consumer not re-rendering). Broke the Provider three ways (actions memo depending on the badges, no progress event, `loadOnMount` ignored); each fails a test, and the no-event break also fails the integration suite's progress-indicator test. (This entry first said `PrDataPolling.test.jsx`'s "fallback when `window.AUTO_*` is unset" test became one for the exported cadences; the edit script silently dropped that change - fixed in the follow-up below.) The rest provide `RequestActivityContext` instead of `window` stubs; the harness's scheduler test calls `renderSchedulerStatus` through a `RequestActivityTestHandle`.
- `lint:check` clean; `test:ci` 2591 passed + 1 skipped (2584 at 7.2, +4 from a parallel commit's `useStableCallback`/`PrTableApp` tests, +6 here, −2 moved); `test:e2e` 59/59 ×2. Windows local run. `app.dispatcher-tick-concurrency` and `app.routes` (server) failed once in a bare parallel `npx jest` run and pass alone (124/124): load flakes, not touched by this change.

## ✅ **Window-bridge elimination, Phase 7.3 follow-up: heap check restored, a dropped test conversion, stale comments (2026-10-22)**

Found reviewing Phases 7.2-7.3.

- **7.3 broke `npm run stability:heap-check`** (from a parallel session's commits on this branch; on-demand only, so no CI caught it). It made the page poll every 300 ms by pinning `window.AUTO_DATA_POLL_MS` with an init script; 7.3 made the interval a `PrDataPolling` constant, so the page polled every 30 s and data changes went unfetched (a 4-cycle run: 3 of 4 unfetched, verdict FAIL). Fix without bringing back a global: `PrDataPolling` takes a `dataPollMs` prop, and `mountAppRoot()` passes `readDataPollMsOverride(window.location.search)`, which accepts `?dataPollMs=` as a whole number ≥ 100 ms (`MIN_DATA_POLL_MS`) and otherwise leaves the default. The heap check opens `/?dataPollMs=300`. Same 4-cycle run: 0 of 4 unfetched.
- **A test conversion 7.3 claimed was silently dropped by its edit script** (an extra-edits callback returned its input string, discarding its own changes). The stale "falls back when `window.AUTO_*` is unset" test is now the exported-cadence test that entry described, plus a `dataPollMs` prop test and 11 `readDataPollMsOverride` cases (13 cases replace 1: net +12). The prop test fails if the prop is ignored. This round's edit scripts re-read each file to check every change landed.
- **Stale comments** still describing deleted bridges (`window.renderSchedulerStatus`/`renderRequestActivity`/`getBackfillTrackingState`/`AUTO_*`/`index.page.js`'s `loadBackfillStatus`) as live: `PrDataPolling.jsx` header, `useQuickCheckActions` (+ test), `useBackfillActions`, `useAckLabelActions`, `useRunScript`, `BackfillLogControls`, `BackfillStatusProvider`, `PrDataProvider`, `index.page.js`, `index.html.test.js`.
- `lint:check` clean; `test:ci` 2603 passed + 1 skipped (2592 + 12 = 2604); `test:e2e` 59/59. Full default heap check (200 cycles, 40 PRs) twice in a row: 1.11x growth, 200 data changes, 0 unfetched (matches the parallel session's post-fix figure). Two earlier full runs failed and aren't explained for certain: one timed out waiting for a tab at cycle ~75 while `test:ci` was running concurrently, and the next timed out on the initial page load; nothing was left listening on its ports, and every run since passed.

## ✅ **Window-bridge elimination, Phase 7.4: the auto-render-blocking state is computed in React (`AutoRenderBlockingSync`); 4 bridges deleted, 3 left test-only (2026-10-22)**

Plan: [WINDOW_BRIDGE_ELIMINATION_PLAN.md](WINDOW_BRIDGE_ELIMINATION_PLAN.md), Phase 7.4 (the auto-render-blocking/notes-dirty/author-insights-drafts cluster).

- **What moved.** `index.page.js`'s `recomputeDirtyPrSectionsFields` worked out which PRs have unsaved Notes edits and which authors have an unsaved manual-comment draft, then pushed that into `AutoRenderBlockingProvider` and set the "jump to unsaved" chips. It read its inputs back from React through four bridges (`getDirtyNotesPrNumbers`, `getAuthorInsightsDraftsSnapshot`, `getAuthorManualCommentsForLogin`, `getReactPrTablePayload`), and `NotesDirtyProvider`/`AuthorInsightsDraftsProvider` called it through `window` on every change. A new headless `components/AutoRenderBlockingSync.jsx` computes the same state with the same pure helpers, reading the inputs from Context, and pushes it through a new `setBlockingState` on `AutoRenderBlockingProvider`'s actions Context.
- **Placement.** The sync needs both `NotesDirtyProvider` (was inside the table portal) and `AuthorInsightsDraftsProvider` (inside `AppRoot`). `NotesDirtyProvider` now wraps `<AppRoot />` from `mountAppRoot()`, and `AppRoot` renders the sync just inside `AuthorInsightsDraftsProvider`. The integration harness nests them the same way (lesson 6).
- **Drafts version Context (lesson 1).** The drafts Provider's Context value is deliberately stable, so it can't signal a change. It now also publishes its write counter on a separate `AuthorInsightsDraftsVersionContext`; the sync is its only reader, so a keystroke in a draft re-renders only the sync. The sync also skips the push when the computed state hasn't changed (most keystrokes), so `AutoRenderBlockingProvider` and its consumers don't re-render per keystroke. (Before, every keystroke re-rendered all of `AppRoot`, through `setAutoRenderBlockedLinks`.)
- **Chips.** `AutoRenderBlockingProvider`'s display value now carries `blockingPrNumbers`/`blockingAuthorInsightsLogins`/`blockingPrLabel`. `react-app.jsx`'s new `AutoRenderBlockedLinksLive` renders the chips from those and sets the chip list's `hidden`/`aria-label`. `AppRoot`'s chip state and `pr-auto-render-indicator-links.helpers.js` (+ its test, 5 tests) are deleted.
- **The `#pr-sections` input/change/click listeners are gone.** They only re-ran the recompute, which re-read the same Context-owned state the sync now subscribes to. Their setTimeout deferral existed so a synchronous Provider update wouldn't revert a controlled input mid-keystroke; the sync runs in an effect, after the input's own onChange has committed. e2e's per-keystroke Rally-input test still covers that bug.
- **Actor identity.** `index.page.js` kept a lazy actor-login alias cache, derived from `window.getReactPrTablePayload()`, for `normalizeActorLogin`. The sync uses `useActorIdentity()` (already alias-aware from the same payload), and orders blocking authors by display name from the payload's `actorsMap`, as before.
- **`index.page.js` rewritten to its live code:** credential hints (+ their `viewprs:react-ready` re-run), `setupMultiSelectDropdownClosing`, the two global error filters. 1191 → 130 lines. The ~1000 lines of history comments about code that has moved are replaced by a short header pointing to this file, the window-bridge plan and git history. `__testables` is gone: `index.page.notifications.test.js` builds the same helpers directly. The dead `statusIcon` destructure went too.
- **Deleted bridges (4):** `window.recomputeDirtyPrSectionsFields`, `pushAutoRenderBlockingState`, `updateReactAutoRenderBlockedLinks`, `getAuthorManualCommentsForLogin`. **Now test-only (3):** `getDirtyNotesPrNumbers`, `getAuthorInsightsDraftsSnapshot` (e2e observers for the unsaved-notes/draft tests) and `getReactPrTablePayload` (integration tests and e2e read the shown payload); left for the test-only step with the others.
- **Tests:** new `AutoRenderBlockingSync.test.jsx` (+7: empty start; dirty notes give ordered PRs + label and hold a pending poll; cleaning flushes the pending payload to the applier; composer draft blocks/unblocks; an edit draft that differs from the saved comment blocks; authors ordered by `actorsMap` display name, not login; unchanged keystrokes don't re-render `AutoRenderBlockingProvider`'s consumers). Broke the sync three ways (no drafts-version dependency, no display-name sort, no equal-state skip); each fails tests (4, 1, 1). `AutoRenderBlockingProvider.test.jsx` pushes through `setBlockingState` (+1 for the chip fields). Drafts/comments tests check the version bump instead of a `window.recomputeDirtyPrSectionsFields` mock (and that the drafts Context value and its consumers don't change). `react-app.test.jsx`'s `viewprs:react-ready` ordering guard checked `window.updateReactAutoRenderBlockedLinks`; AppRoot publishes no bridge now, so it checks that the portaled `#repo` input (what the remaining listener needs) is in the DOM, and fails when it isn't. The integration suite's two deferred-poll tests mark notes dirty through a `NotesDirtyTestHandle` on the real Provider, instead of stubbing `window.getDirtyNotesPrNumbers` and calling `window.recomputeDirtyPrSectionsFields`.
- `lint:check` clean; `test:ci` 2606 passed + 1 skipped (2604 + 8 − 5); `test:e2e` 59/59 ×2 (2.0 min each). Windows local run. Two earlier full runs failed 9 and 7 tests, all timeouts, while another local session was running a CPU stress test (a busy loop on every core, CPU at 100%). Under that load an unmodified worktree of `7156b89` failed the same opening tests; once the load stopped, the same 7-test sequence passed on both trees, then the full suite passed twice.

## ✅ **Window-bridge elimination, Phase 7.4 follow-up: the chip list's attributes get tests again; stale comments (2026-10-22)**

Found reviewing Phase 7.4.

- **Coverage gap.** 7.4 moved the chip list's `hidden`/`aria-label` logic into a private `AutoRenderBlockedLinksLive` in `react-app.jsx` and deleted the helper whose 5 tests covered it, leaving it untested (e2e only proved the list un-hides, by clicking a chip). It's now exported from `AutoRenderBlockedLinks.jsx`, and `AutoRenderBlockedLinks.test.jsx` has 4 tests against a real `AutoRenderBlockingProvider`: hidden with an empty label when nothing blocks; unhidden, labelled with the PR label, one chip per PR; author-only label lists display names; hidden again once the state is empty. Broke it two ways (never hidden; no display names), and each fails tests (2, 1).
- **Stale comments:** `AutoRenderBlockingProvider.jsx`'s header still said its state is "pushed in from vanilla/other-Provider callers via a bridge" and read through "read bridges" (both are the actions Context now); an `index.html.test.js` comment cited the deleted `renderAutoRenderBlockedIndicator`.
- `lint:check` clean; `test:ci` 2610 passed + 1 skipped (2607 + 4); `test:e2e` 59/59; `vite build` succeeds. Windows local run.

## ✅ **Window-bridge elimination, Phase 7.5: `index.page.js` deleted (2026-10-22)**

Plan: [WINDOW_BRIDGE_ELIMINATION_PLAN.md](WINDOW_BRIDGE_ELIMINATION_PLAN.md), Phase 7's end state ("`index.page.js` is at most the entry calling `mountAppRoot()`, or deleted").

- **Credential-field hints are JSX props now.** `index.page.js` ran `applyCredentialHints` over six inputs by DOM id: `repo`, `limit`, `merged-limit`, `jobs`, `pr-numbers` (all `ContextRunScriptTextInput`) and `filter-pr-numbers` (`PrNumberFilterInput`). React renders all six, so the pass did nothing on its first run and had to re-run on `viewprs:react-ready`. Both components now spread a shared, frozen `NON_CREDENTIAL_INPUT_PROPS` (`form-parsing.helpers.js`), which React renders as the same attributes. `applyCredentialHints` is deleted.
- **Dropdown closing** is `state/useMultiSelectDropdownClosing.jsx`, called from `AppRoot`, with cleanup. It looks up the dropdowns on each click instead of once at setup; all 9 are static `index.html` markup, so it's the same set. `pr-filter-panel.helpers.js` (+ its 3 tests) is deleted.
- **The two global error filters** (swallowing password managers' `ControlLooksLikePasswordCredentialField`/`UsernameElementUniqueID` errors) are `helpers/credential-field-error-filter.helpers.js`, installed by `react-app.jsx` just before `mountAppRoot()`, the same point in startup.
- **`viewprs:react-ready` is gone**, with its dispatch effect in `AppRoot` and `react-app.test.jsx`'s ordering guard. The hints were its last listener. `index.html` no longer loads `index.page.js`; the integration harness no longer `require()`s it; it's off eslint's ESM list.
- **Tests:** new `credential-field-error-filter.helpers.test.js` (+10: classification cases; credential-field error/rejection suppressed without logging; other errors left alone and logged; uninstall) and `useMultiSelectDropdownClosing.test.jsx` (+4: outside click closes all; inside click keeps that one; dropdown added later still closes; unmount removes the listener). `form-parsing.helpers.test.js`: the 5 `applyCredentialHints` tests become 2 for the props (rendered attributes match; frozen). `ContextRunScriptTextInput`/`PrNumberFilterInput` tests +1 each for the attributes; `react-app.test.jsx` +2 (portaled `#repo` has the hints; the installed filter suppresses a credential-field error and not others). **Deleted 2 integration tests** that checked the hints on the harness's own stand-in inputs (`injectRunFilterFieldElements`), not the real components; they passed only because `index.page.js` patched that markup. The components' tests, `react-app.test.jsx` and e2e's six-field browser test cover the real inputs. Net 2611 → 2621.
- **Break checks:** props removed from `ContextRunScriptTextInput` (2 fail), dropdowns never closing (3 fail), filters not installed at startup (1 fail). **An incident during these:** the break-check script backed files up with `cp`, which is aliased here to prompt before overwriting (lesson 4). The second and third backups were refused, so "restoring" wrote `ContextRunScriptTextInput.jsx`'s content over `useMultiSelectDropdownClosing.jsx` and `react-app.jsx`. Both were rebuilt (the hook from the version written earlier in the session, `react-app.jsx` from HEAD plus this phase's four edits, verified against `git diff`), and the filter break check, which had run against the broken file, was redone with a per-file `cat` backup.
- `lint:check` clean; `test:ci` 2620 passed + 1 skipped; `test:e2e` 59/59; `vite build` succeeds. Windows local run, machine idle.
